statement = <sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql.base.PGCompiler object at 0x0000021B546E0190>
parameters = [{}]

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Unpack[TupleAny]]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
                for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                    str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                        self,
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                        context.executemany,
                    )
    
            if self._echo:
                self._log_info(str_statement)
    
                stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
                self._log_parameters(
                    effective_parameters, stats, ismulti=context.executemany
                )
    
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

statement = <sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql.base.PGCompiler object at 0x0000021B546E0190>
parameters = [{}]

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Unpack[TupleAny]]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
                for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                    str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                        self,
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                        context.executemany,
                    )
    
            if self._echo:
                self._log_info(str_statement)
    
                stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
                self._log_parameters(
                    effective_parameters, stats, ismulti=context.executemany
                )
    
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

statement = <sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql.base.PGCompiler object at 0x0000021B546E0190>
parameters = [{}]

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Unpack[TupleAny]]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
                for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                    str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                        self,
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                        context.executemany,
                    )
    
            if self._echo:
                self._log_info(str_statement)
    
                stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
                self._log_parameters(
                    effective_parameters, stats, ismulti=context.executemany
                )
    
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

_______ test_verified_keys_resolve_only_when_a_district_is_unambiguous ________

session = <sqlmodel.orm.session.Session object at 0x0000021B6BD7D4F0>

    def test_verified_keys_resolve_only_when_a_district_is_unambiguous(session):
        """The verified key is a DOSM district name or the block stays unavailable.
    
        Resolution is exact-name and requires the name to be published by exactly
        one state, so a duplicated or absent district can never pull in another
        region's demographics.
        """
        rows = session.exec(
            text(
                "SELECT DISTINCT p.dosm_district_key, "
                "  (SELECT count(DISTINCT d.state) FROM ("
                "     SELECT state, district FROM public.district_population "
                "     UNION SELECT state, district FROM public.district_age_population "
                "     UNION SELECT state, district FROM public.hh_income_district"
                "   ) d WHERE d.district = p.dosm_district_key) AS states "
                "FROM fyp_features.property_population_spatial p "
                "WHERE p.source_record_type = 'SITEWISE_U_PROPERTY' "
                "  AND p.geography_match_status = 'MATCHED' "
                "  AND p.dosm_district_key IS NOT NULL"

    def test_cold_process_returns_pending_score_and_never_blocks(live_service, monkeypatch):
        service, operational = live_service
        _cleanup_rows()
        _clear_in_memory()
    
        # Simulate a freshly started process whose one-off SHAP warm-up has not
        # completed: the request path must not compute SHAP.
        saved_flag = shap_explainer._WARM_FLAG
        shap_explainer._WARM_FLAG = type(saved_flag)()
        try:
            started = time.perf_counter()
            out = service.insights(REFERENCE_PROPERTY)
            elapsed = time.perf_counter() - started
        finally:
            shap_explainer._WARM_FLAG = saved_flag

statement = <sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql.base.PGCompiler object at 0x0000021B51B34B90>
parameters = [{'id': 'MD-00510'}]

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Unpack[TupleAny]]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
                for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                    str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                        self,
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                        context.executemany,
                    )
    
            if self._echo:
                self._log_info(str_statement)
    
                stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
                self._log_parameters(
                    effective_parameters, stats, ismulti=context.executemany
                )
    
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Unpack[TupleAny]]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
                for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                    str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                        self,
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                        context.executemany,
                    )
    
            if self._echo:
                self._log_info(str_statement)
    
                stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
                self._log_parameters(
                    effective_parameters, stats, ismulti=context.executemany
                )
    
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    def test_enrichment_is_idempotent(client, owner_token, created_properties):
        _sub, token = owner_token
        created = client.post(
            "/api/v1/properties/listings", headers=auth(token), json=PROPERTY_BODY
        )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Unpack[TupleAny]]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
                for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                    str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                        self,
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                        context.executemany,
                    )
    
            if self._echo:
                self._log_info(str_statement)
    
                stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
                self._log_parameters(
                    effective_parameters, stats, ismulti=context.executemany
                )
    
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:

                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )

                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Unpack[TupleAny]]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
                for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                    str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                        self,
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                        context.executemany,
                    )
    
            if self._echo:
                self._log_info(str_statement)
    
                stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
                self._log_parameters(
                    effective_parameters, stats, ismulti=context.executemany
                )
    
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Unpack[TupleAny]]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
                for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                    str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                        self,
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                        context.executemany,
                    )
    
            if self._echo:
                self._log_info(str_statement)
    
                stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
                self._log_parameters(
                    effective_parameters, stats, ismulti=context.executemany
                )
    
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    def test_synchronizer_inserts_updates_and_is_idempotent_in_temp_table():
        """Exercises the real INSERT/UPDATE SQL against a temp clone of the
        outlet table, so no production competitor row is touched."""
        from sitewise.ingestion.synchronizer import OutletSynchronizer

make_property = <function make_property.<locals>._make at 0x0000021B524AF060>
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x0000021B5238A3C0>

    def test_category_failure_does_not_stop_the_others(make_property, monkeypatch):
        """A raising category settles as ``failed`` while the rest stay current."""
        pid = make_property()
    
        def boom(*_args, **_kwargs):
            raise RuntimeError("parking source unavailable")
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(PropertyEnrichmentService, "_parking", boom)
        with Session(engine) as session:
            summary = PropertyEnrichmentService(session).run(pid, phase="postgis")
    
        states = summary["categories"]
        assert states["parking"]["status"] == "failed"
        assert "parking source unavailable" in states["parking"]["last_error"]
        for category in ("fnb_competitors", "demand_generators", "roads_transport"):

make_property = <function make_property.<locals>._make at 0x0000021B7548F740>

    def test_an_abandoned_running_job_is_reclaimed(make_property):
        pid = make_property()
        with Session(engine) as session:
            service = PropertyEnrichmentService(session)
            service.ensure_schema()
            session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO fyp.property_enrichment_category
                        (property_id, category, phase, status, attempt_count,
                         started_at, updated_at)
                    VALUES (:pid, 'fnb_competitors', 'postgis', 'running', 1,
                            now() - interval '2 hours', now())
                    ON CONFLICT (property_id, category) DO UPDATE
                       SET status = 'running',
                           started_at = now() - interval '2 hours'
                    """
                ),
                {"pid": pid},
            )
            session.commit()
    
            service.run(pid, phase="postgis")
            state = service.states(pid)["fnb_competitors"]

    def test_summary_reports_progress_and_readiness(make_property):
        pid = make_property()

src\sitewise\services\property_insights_service.py:718: in insights
    merged, _payload, _key = self._insights_full(

self = <sitewise.services.property_insights_service.PropertyInsightsService object at 0x0000021B75518180>
property_id = 'MD-00510', origin = None, business_type = None
cache_session = None

    def _insights_full(
        self,
        property_id: str,
        origin: dict[str, float] | None = None,
        business_type: str | None = None,
        *,
        cache_session: Session | None | object = _UNSET,
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any] | None, str | None]:
        """Build the full insights read model.
    
        ``cache_session`` defaults to the constructor-provided operational
        session (``self.cache_session``); pass ``None`` explicitly to force
        the historical in-memory-only behaviour (used by the worker's compute
        pass and the precompute CLI, which persist under their own identity).
    
        Returns ``(merged_response, cached_payload, persistent_cache_key)``.
        ``cached_payload`` is the persistable explanation portion (score,
        explanation, categories, map, availability) when one was resolved this
        call, and ``persistent_cache_key`` the identity it belongs to. The
        optional ``cache_session`` (operational database) enables the
        persistent validated-explanation cache; without it the service keeps
        its historical behaviour: in-memory cache plus synchronous computation
        on a miss.
    
        Contract (unchanged by the performance work):
    
        * Returns the active PU score independently of optional SHAP. The
          score and its explanation are separate products: a missing feature,
          missing SHAP background or SHAP failure must not hide a valid stored
          or live PU score. The catalogue ranking's legacy score remains
          authoritative when the active variant is ``legacy``; any available
          live inference is compared with it rather than silently substituted.
        *         ``business_type`` is the entrepreneur's own selection. It selects
        which of the four category outputs the Stage-2 explanation describes
        — never the model's top category, unless no selection is known.
        * With the persistent cache enabled, a validated stored explanation is
          served immediately; on a miss the request either computes
          synchronously (only when this process has completed its one-off SHAP
          warm-up — measured, not assumed) or returns the real PU score with a
          truthful ``pending`` status and schedules the computation on the
          background worker. A blocking multi-second SHAP computation never
          runs inside a request.
        """
        if cache_session is _UNSET:
            cache_session = self.cache_session
        selected_category = match_business_type(business_type)
        variant = self._variant()
        base: dict[str, Any] = {
            "property_id": property_id,
            "model": variant,
            "origin": origin,
            "map": {"layers": _layer_payloads({}, measured=False)},
            "panorama": self._panorama(property_id),
            "score": {"available": False, "label": "Location Fit", "meaning": SCORE_MEANING},
            "explanation": {"available": False},
            "business_type": {
                "requested": business_type,
                "matched_category": selected_category,
                "matched": selected_category is not None,
            },
            "categories": [
                {
                    **{k: spec[k] for k in ("key", "label", "question", "why")},
                    "available": False,
                    "contribution": 0.0,
                    "direction": "neutral",
                    "feature_count": 0,
                    "factors": [],
                    "note": None,
                }
                for spec in CATEGORY_SPECS
            ],
            "availability": {
                "score": False,
                "explanation": False,
                "factors": False,
                "categories": False,
            },
        }
    
        if self.analytics is None:
            base["reason"] = {
                "code": "analytics_unavailable",
                "message": "Location analysis is not configured for this deployment.",
            }
            return base, None, None
    
        # Stage 2 is independent of the PU variant: the category model has its
        # own 54-feature contract, its own validation and its own wording. A
        # PU failure must never hide it, and vice versa.
>       base["category_model"] = self._category_payload(property_id, selected_category)
                                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       TypeError: _service.<locals>.<lambda>() takes 2 positional arguments but 3 were given

property_id = 'MD-00510', origin = None, business_type = None
cache_session = None

    def _insights_full(
        self,
        property_id: str,
        origin: dict[str, float] | None = None,
        business_type: str | None = None,
        *,
        cache_session: Session | None | object = _UNSET,
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any] | None, str | None]:
        """Build the full insights read model.
    
        ``cache_session`` defaults to the constructor-provided operational
        session (``self.cache_session``); pass ``None`` explicitly to force
        the historical in-memory-only behaviour (used by the worker's compute
        pass and the precompute CLI, which persist under their own identity).
    
        Returns ``(merged_response, cached_payload, persistent_cache_key)``.
        ``cached_payload`` is the persistable explanation portion (score,
        explanation, categories, map, availability) when one was resolved this
        call, and ``persistent_cache_key`` the identity it belongs to. The
        optional ``cache_session`` (operational database) enables the
        persistent validated-explanation cache; without it the service keeps
        its historical behaviour: in-memory cache plus synchronous computation
        on a miss.
    
        Contract (unchanged by the performance work):
    
        * Returns the active PU score independently of optional SHAP. The
          score and its explanation are separate products: a missing feature,
          missing SHAP background or SHAP failure must not hide a valid stored
          or live PU score. The catalogue ranking's legacy score remains
          authoritative when the active variant is ``legacy``; any available
          live inference is compared with it rather than silently substituted.
        *         ``business_type`` is the entrepreneur's own selection. It selects
        which of the four category outputs the Stage-2 explanation describes
        — never the model's top category, unless no selection is known.
        * With the persistent cache enabled, a validated stored explanation is
          served immediately; on a miss the request either computes
          synchronously (only when this process has completed its one-off SHAP
          warm-up — measured, not assumed) or returns the real PU score with a
          truthful ``pending`` status and schedules the computation on the
          background worker. A blocking multi-second SHAP computation never
          runs inside a request.
        """
        if cache_session is _UNSET:
            cache_session = self.cache_session
        selected_category = match_business_type(business_type)
        variant = self._variant()
        base: dict[str, Any] = {
            "property_id": property_id,
            "model": variant,
            "origin": origin,
            "map": {"layers": _layer_payloads({}, measured=False)},
            "panorama": self._panorama(property_id),
            "score": {"available": False, "label": "Location Fit", "meaning": SCORE_MEANING},
            "explanation": {"available": False},
            "business_type": {
                "requested": business_type,
                "matched_category": selected_category,
                "matched": selected_category is not None,
            },
            "categories": [
                {
                    **{k: spec[k] for k in ("key", "label", "question", "why")},
                    "available": False,
                    "contribution": 0.0,
                    "direction": "neutral",
                    "feature_count": 0,
                    "factors": [],
                    "note": None,
                }
                for spec in CATEGORY_SPECS
            ],
            "availability": {
                "score": False,
                "explanation": False,
                "factors": False,
                "categories": False,
            },
        }
    
        if self.analytics is None:
            base["reason"] = {
                "code": "analytics_unavailable",
                "message": "Location analysis is not configured for this deployment.",
            }
            return base, None, None
    
        # Stage 2 is independent of the PU variant: the category model has its
        # own 54-feature contract, its own validation and its own wording. A
        # PU failure must never hide it, and vice versa.
>       base["category_model"] = self._category_payload(property_id, selected_category)
                                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       TypeError: _service.<locals>.<lambda>() takes 2 positional arguments but 3 were given

    def test_explanation_is_cached_per_model_version(monkeypatch, reference_features, background):
        calls = {"n": 0}
        real_explain = svc.ShapExplainer.explain
    
        def counting(self, features, **kwargs):
            calls["n"] += 1
            return real_explain(self, features, **kwargs)
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(svc.ShapExplainer, "explain", counting)
    
        service = _service(monkeypatch, features=reference_features, background_rows=background)
>       first = service.insights(REFERENCE_PROPERTY_ID)
                ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

tests\test_property_insights_service.py:371: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _
src\sitewise\services\property_insights_service.py:718: in insights
    merged, _payload, _key = self._insights_full(
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _

self = <sitewise.services.property_insights_service.PropertyInsightsService object at 0x0000021B52477BD0>
property_id = 'MD-00510', origin = None, business_type = None
cache_session = None

    def _insights_full(
        self,
        property_id: str,
        origin: dict[str, float] | None = None,
        business_type: str | None = None,
        *,
        cache_session: Session | None | object = _UNSET,
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any] | None, str | None]:
        """Build the full insights read model.
    
        ``cache_session`` defaults to the constructor-provided operational
        session (``self.cache_session``); pass ``None`` explicitly to force
        the historical in-memory-only behaviour (used by the worker's compute
        pass and the precompute CLI, which persist under their own identity).
    
        Returns ``(merged_response, cached_payload, persistent_cache_key)``.
        ``cached_payload`` is the persistable explanation portion (score,
        explanation, categories, map, availability) when one was resolved this
        call, and ``persistent_cache_key`` the identity it belongs to. The
        optional ``cache_session`` (operational database) enables the
        persistent validated-explanation cache; without it the service keeps
        its historical behaviour: in-memory cache plus synchronous computation
        on a miss.
    
        Contract (unchanged by the performance work):
    
        * Returns the active PU score independently of optional SHAP. The
          score and its explanation are separate products: a missing feature,
          missing SHAP background or SHAP failure must not hide a valid stored
          or live PU score. The catalogue ranking's legacy score remains
          authoritative when the active variant is ``legacy``; any available
          live inference is compared with it rather than silently substituted.
        *         ``business_type`` is the entrepreneur's own selection. It selects
        which of the four category outputs the Stage-2 explanation describes
        — never the model's top category, unless no selection is known.
        * With the persistent cache enabled, a validated stored explanation is
          served immediately; on a miss the request either computes
          synchronously (only when this process has completed its one-off SHAP
          warm-up — measured, not assumed) or returns the real PU score with a
          truthful ``pending`` status and schedules the computation on the
          background worker. A blocking multi-second SHAP computation never
          runs inside a request.
        """
        if cache_session is _UNSET:
            cache_session = self.cache_session
        selected_category = match_business_type(business_type)
        variant = self._variant()
        base: dict[str, Any] = {
            "property_id": property_id,
            "model": variant,
            "origin": origin,
            "map": {"layers": _layer_payloads({}, measured=False)},
            "panorama": self._panorama(property_id),
            "score": {"available": False, "label": "Location Fit", "meaning": SCORE_MEANING},
            "explanation": {"available": False},
            "business_type": {
                "requested": business_type,
                "matched_category": selected_category,
                "matched": selected_category is not None,
            },
            "categories": [
                {
                    **{k: spec[k] for k in ("key", "label", "question", "why")},
                    "available": False,
                    "contribution": 0.0,
                    "direction": "neutral",
                    "feature_count": 0,
                    "factors": [],
                    "note": None,
                }
                for spec in CATEGORY_SPECS
            ],
            "availability": {
                "score": False,
                "explanation": False,
                "factors": False,
                "categories": False,
            },
        }
    
        if self.analytics is None:
            base["reason"] = {
                "code": "analytics_unavailable",
                "message": "Location analysis is not configured for this deployment.",
            }
            return base, None, None
    
        # Stage 2 is independent of the PU variant: the category model has its
        # own 54-feature contract, its own validation and its own wording. A
        # PU failure must never hide it, and vice versa.
>       base["category_model"] = self._category_payload(property_id, selected_category)
                                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       TypeError: _service.<locals>.<lambda>() takes 2 positional arguments but 3 were given

    def test_map_exposes_the_five_layers_in_display_order(monkeypatch, reference_features, background):
        service = _service(monkeypatch, features=reference_features, background_rows=background)

    def _category_payload(
        self, property_id: str, business_type: str | None = None
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """The F&B category block for the entrepreneur's SELECTED category.
    
        Uses the category model's OWN 54-feature contract (leakage-reduced:
        the ten competitor-count columns are excluded) and its own SHAP
        background. A category score is never explained with the PU
        explanation, and vice versa. When ``business_type`` is known the block
        reports and explains THAT class; the model's own top class is reported
        separately and never substituted for the entrepreneur's choice.
        Weak validation is reported with every result so the UI can keep the
        exploratory wording.
        """
        try:
            from sitewise.ml import category_aware
    
            bundle = category_aware.load_bundle()
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - evidence is additive only
            return {
                "available": False,
                "reason": {"code": "category_model_unavailable", "message": str(exc)},
            }
    
        # The Stage-2 explanation is also a permutation-SHAP computation. On a
        # cold process (one-off SHAP compilation still running off-path) it
        # must not block the request either: report truthfully and let the
        # next refresh pick the warmed result up.
        if self.cache_session is not None and not _shap_module.process_warm():
            return {
                "available": False,
                "model_version": bundle.version,
                "status": "pending",
                "reason": {
                    "code": "category_model_pending",
                    "message": (
                        "The category explanation engine is still warming up "
                        "in this freshly started backend process; please "
                        "refresh in a few seconds."
                    ),
                },
            }
    
        version = bundle.version
        names = list(bundle.category_features)
        context_names = [n for n in COMPETITOR_CONTEXT_FEATURES if n not in names]
        required = [*names, *context_names]
        try:
            feature_bundle = PropertyFeatureRepository(self.analytics).get(
                property_id, required, source="catalog"
            )
            coverage = merge_bridge_features(feature_bundle, expected_features=required)
            coverage = merge_expanded_bridge_features(
                coverage, expected_features=required
            )
        except FeatureResolutionError as exc:
            return {
                "available": False,
                "model_version": version,
                "reason": {"code": "features_unresolved", "message": str(exc)},
            }
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - fail closed, never fabricate
            return {
                "available": False,
                "model_version": version,
                "reason": {
                    "code": "features_unresolved",
                    "message": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
                },
            }
    
        values = dict(coverage.values)
        cache_key = (
            f"{version}:{bundle.sha256[:16]}:feat:{_feature_digest(values)}:"
            f"bt:{business_type or 'top'}",
            property_id,
        )
        with _CATEGORY_CACHE_LOCK:
            cached = _CATEGORY_CACHE.get(cache_key)
        if cached is not None:
            return cached
    
        try:
            result = predict_categories(values, business_type=business_type)
        except ModelUnavailableError as exc:
            return {
                "available": False,
                "model_version": version,
                "reason": {
                    "code": "category_inference_unavailable",
                    "message": str(exc),
                    "missing_features": [n for n in names if values.get(n) is None],
                },
            }
    
        result["property_id"] = property_id
        result["competition_context_NOT_used_by_model"] = competition_context(values)
        result["feature_coverage"] = {
            "available_features": sum(1 for n in names if values.get(n) is not None),
            "feature_count": len(names),
            "missing_features": [n for n in names if values.get(n) is None],
            "flags": list(coverage.flags),
        }
        result["business_type"] = {
            "requested": business_type,
            "matched_category": business_type,
            "matched": business_type is not None,
        }
        if any(values.get(n) is None for n in names):
            result["explanation"] = {
                "available": False,
                "state": "withheld_imputed_features",
                "reason": {
                    "code": "category_shap_imputed",
                    "message": (
                        "The displayed category scores use the artifact's median "
                        "imputation for missing residential measurements, so no "
                        "SHAP explanation is shown."
                    ),
                },
            }
        else:
            try:
